				\node (A-label) at ($(A)+(90:2.5)$) {\(A\)};
				\node[LavenderMagenta] (SA) at ($(mpA-b)!0.5!(mpA-c)$) {\(S\cap A\)};
				\draw ($(SA)+(0:0.7)$) edge[edge,LavenderMagenta,closed,curve through={($(SA)+(45:1)$) ($(SA)+(90:0.7)$) ($(SA)+(135:0.7)$) ($(SA)+(180:0.7)$) ($(SA)+(225:1)$) ($(SA)+(270:0.7)$)}] ($(SA)+(315:0.7)$);
				\draw ($(mpA-a)+(90:0.4)$) edge[edge,dashed,closed,curve through={($(mpA-a)+(0:0.4)$) ($(v)+(90:0.4)$) ($(v)+(0:0.2)$) ($(v)+(270:0.4)$) ($(mpA-b)+(270:1.3)$) ($(mpA-c)+(270:0.7)$) ($(mpA-c)+(180:0.7)$)}] ($(mpA-b)+(90:1)$);
				
				\node (mpA-label) at ($(A)+(260:1.2)$) {\(\mu'(A)\)};
				
				  
				\node[vertex,AppleGreen] (w) at ($(Ap)+(20:2)$) {};
				\node[AppleGreen] (w-label) at ($(w)+(90:0.3)$) {\(w\)};
				
				\node[vertex,AppleGreen] (z) at ($(v)+(70:0.6)$) {};
				\node (zp) at ($(z)+(350:0.6)$) {};
				\node[AppleGreen] (z-label) at ($(z)+(280:0.3)$) {\(z\)};
				
				    
				\node (App-a) at ($(Ap)+(140:1.3)$) {};
				\node (App-b) at ($(Ap)+(240:1.5)$) {};
				\node[violet] (App-label) at ($(App-b)$) {\(A''\)};
				\draw ($(App-a)+(90:0.4)$) edge[edge,violet,closed,curve through={($(App-a)!0.5!(w)$) ($(w)+(90:0.65)$) ($(w)+(0:0.4)$) ($(w)+(270:0.6)$) ($(App-b)+(10:0.3)$) ($(App-b)+(270:0.3)$) ($(App-b)+(190:0.3)$) ($(v)+(270:0.3)$) ($(v)+(180:0.3)$) ($(z)+(130:0.3)$) ($(z)+(90:0.4)$)}] ($(App-a)+(135:0.6)$);
				
				
				        
				\node (mpAp-c) at ($(w)+(260:2)$) {};
				\node (mpAp-b) at ($(mpAp-c)+(200:1)$) {};
				\node (mpAp-a) at ($(mpAp-b)+(220:1.3)$) {};
				\draw ($(w)+(90:0.8)$) edge[edge,CornflowerBlue,dashed,closed,curve through={($(w)+(0:0.7)$) ($(w)!0.5!(mpAp-c)+(315:0.9)$) ($(mpAp-c)+(350:0.7)$) ($(mpAp-c)+(270:0.6)$) ($(mpAp-b)+(270:0.2)$) ($(mpAp-b)+(180:0.2)$) ($(App-b)+(70:0.3)$) ($(v)+(270:0.2)$) ($(v)+(180:0.2)$) ($(z)+(130:0.2)$) ($(z)+(90:0.2)$)}] ($(App-a)$);
				\node[CornflowerBlue] (mpAp-label) at ($(mpAp-b)+(100:0.5)$) {\(\mu'(A')\)};
				
				    
				\draw ($(w)+(90:0.6)$) edge[edge,LavenderMagenta,closed,curve through={($(w)+(0:0.5)$) ($(w)!0.5!(mpAp-c)+(315:0.7)$) ($(mpAp-c)+(350:0.4)$) ($(mpAp-c)+(250:0.3)$) ($(mpAp-c)+(160:0.5)$)}] ($(w)+(180:0.5)$);
				
				
				  
				\node (E-d) at ($(v)+(250:1)$) {};
				\node[PastelOrange] (E-label) at ($(App-b)+(200:1)$) {\(E\)};
				\draw ($(w)+(90:0.8)$) edge[edge,PastelOrange,closed,curve through={($(w)+(0:0.6)$) ($(w)+(270:0.75)$) ($(mpAp-b)+(350:0.2)$) ($(mpAp-a)+(0:0.5)$) ($(mpAp-a)+(270:0.2)$) ($(mpAp-a)+(190:0.3)$) ($(App-b)+(280:0.7)$) ($(App-b)+(225:0.7)$) ($(App-b)+(170:0.7)$) ($(E-d)+(270:0.2)$) ($(E-d)+(180:0.2)$) ($(z)+(130:0.5)$) }] ($(App-a)+(90:0.8)$);
				
				
				      
				\draw[edge,AppleGreen,out=45,in=170] (v) to (z);
				\draw[directededge,AppleGreen,out=350,in=180] (z) to (zp.east);
				\draw (zp) edge[edge,AppleGreen,quick curve through={($(zp)+(0:0.2)$) .. ($(v)!0.5!(Ap)+(90:0.3)$) .. ($(Ap)+(90:0.6)$) .. ($(w)!0.5!(Ap)+(90:0.3)$)}] (w);
				\draw[directededge,AppleGreen] ($(w)+(185:0.3)$) to (w);
				
				\draw (w) edge[edge,PastelOrange,quick curve through={($(w)+(150:0.4)$) .. ($(Ap)$) .. ($(v)+(350:0.7)$)}] (v.east);
				\draw[directededge,PastelOrange] ($(v)+(350:0.3)$) to (v);
				
				\node[LavenderMagenta,fill=white,rounded corners] (SAp) at ($(mpAp-c)+(82:0.6)$) {\(S\cap A'\)};
				
				\node[PastelOrange] (Q) at ($(Ap)+(315:0.3)$) {\(Q\)};

			\caption{Depiction of objects used in the proof of~\cref{cl:incomparable}.}
			\label{fig:incomparable}
		\end{figure}
		Refer to~\cref{fig:incomparable} for an illustration of the objects used in this proof.
		
		Suppose for the sake of contradiction that \(v\) is an element of \(\mu(A) \cap \mu(A')\).
		Without loss of generality, assume that \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\).
		From the definition of closure,
		it follows that there is a path \(P\) from \(v\) to an element \(w \in S \cap A'\) contained
		entirely within \(D[\mu'(A')]\) and a path \(Q\) from \(w\) to \(v\) contained
		entirely within \(D[\mu'(A')]\).
		Since \(S \cap A \cap A' = \emptyset\) (as $(A,B),(A',B')$ are incomparable in the tree \(T\)),
		and \(v \in \mu(A) \subseteq A\), the path \(P\) starts in a vertex
		of \(A\), and ends in a vertex not in \(A\). So it must contain a vertex
		\(z \in Z(A)\). Note that we have \(z \not \in S \cap A\), as \(P\)
		is contained within \(\mu'(A') \subseteq A'\) and \(A' \cap S \cap A = \emptyset\).
		
		
		Observe that \((A',B')\) is a \(\Set{z}\)-\((S \cap A)\)-cut, as \(z \in A'\) and \(S \cap A \cap A' = \emptyset\). 
		Define $A''$ to be:
		\begin{itemize}
			\item  the set of all vertices reachable from \(\Set{z}\) within \(D[A']\), if \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\);
			\item the set of all vertices that can reach \(\Set{z}\) within \(D[A']\), if \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\).
		\end{itemize}
		In either case we have $\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A''\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A'' \right)}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A'' \right)}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A'' \right)}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A'' \right)}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}}
                     
	\fi} \leqslant k$ or $\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A''\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A'' \right)}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A'' \right)}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A'' \right)}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A'' \right)}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}}
                     
	\fi} \leqslant k$, respectively, and
		$(A'',V(D)- A'')$ is still a \(\Set{z}\)-\((S \cap A)\)-cut. Moreover, the paths
		\(P\) and \(Q\) are contained within \(D[A'']\) regardless of the orientation
		of the cut \((A', B')\); in particular \(v \in A''\).
		
		Let \((E,F)\) be a $k$-key \(\Set{z}\)-\((S \cap A)\)-cut that
		extends the cut \((A'', V(D) - A'')\) (so with \(A'' \subseteq E\)).
		The paths \(P\) and \(Q\) show that \(v\) belongs to
		\(\ensuremath{\text{closure}_{D[E]}\Paren*{\set{z}}}\), and so \(v\) does
		not belong to \(\mu'(A)\) by construction of $\mu'(A)$. This is a contradiction.
	\end{claimproof}
	
	
	
	\begin{claim}\label{claim:potato_nesting}
		Let \((A,B), (A',B') \in \mathcal{F}\).                                             
		If \((A,B)\) is a descendant of \((A', B')\) in \(T\), and
		\(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}
                     
	\fi} = \Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\) or \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}
                     
	\fi} = \Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\),
		then \(\mu(A) \subseteq \mu(A')\).
	\end{claim}
	\begin{claimproof}
		\begin{figure}[!ht]
			\centering
                                                             
			\begin{tikzpicture}
				\node (Ap) at (0,0) {};
				
				\node[vertex,AppleGreen] (zA) at ($(Ap)+(200:1)$) {};
				\node[AppleGreen] (zA-label) at ($(zA)+(180:0.4)$) {\(z_A\)};
				
				\node[vertex,AppleGreen] (zAp) at ($(Ap)+(270:3)$) {};
				\node[AppleGreen] (zAp-label) at ($(zAp)+(190:0.4)$) {\(z_{A'}\)};
				
				\node (Ap-z) at ($(Ap)+(190:4)$) {};
				
				\node[vertex,violet] (w) at ($(Ap)+(80:1)$) {};
				\node[violet] (w-label) at ($(w)+(30:0.5)$) {\(w\)};
				\node (w-n) at ($(w)+(70:0.6)$) {};
				\node (w-s) at ($(w)+(0:0.6)$) {};
				\node (w-np) at ($(w-n)+(100:1.4)$) {};
				\node (w-sp) at ($(w-s)+(335:0.6)$) {};
				
				  
				\node (A-a) at ($(zA)+(170:0.7)$) {};
				\node (A-x) at ($(Ap)+(355:1)$) {};
				\node (A-b) at ($(w)+(135:2)$) {};
				\node (A-c) at ($(w)+(40:3)$) {};
				
				   
				\node (Ep-c) at ($(zAp)+(270:0.7)$) {};
				\node (Ep-b) at ($(Ep-c)+(180:1)$) {};
				\node (Ep-a) at ($(Ep-b)+(170:1)$) {};
				
				  
				\node (E-a) at ($(Ap-z)+(225:2)$) {};
				\node (E-b) at ($(Ep-c)+(355:2)$) {};
				
				    
				\node (SA-b) at ($(A-c)+(290:2)$) {};
				
				     
				\node (mpAp-y) at ($(A-x)+(315:1)$) {};
				
				         
				\draw ($(zAp)+(270:0.4)$) edge[edge,CornflowerBlue,closed,curve through={ ($(Ep-b)+(45:0.4)$) ($(Ep-a)+(45:0.4)$) ($(Ap-z)+(200:0.4)$) ($(A-b)+(135:0.6)$) ($(A-c)+(30:1)$)}] ($(SA-b)+(0:1)$);
				\node[CornflowerBlue] (Ap-label) at ($(A-c)+(45:1.3)$) {\(A'\)};
				
				        
				\draw ($(Ap)+(270:0.4)$) edge[edge,closed,curve through={ ($(zA)+(270:0.4)$) ($(A-a)+(225:0.4)$) ($(A-b)+(135:0.4)$) ($(A-b)!0.5!(A-c)+(90:0.8)$) ($(A-c)+(30:0.6)$) ($(w-sp)+(335:0.2)$)}] (A-x);
				\node (A-label) at ($(A-a)+(90:2)$) {\(A\)};
				
				       
				\draw ($(w)+(225:0.3)$) edge[edge,dashed,closed,curve through={ ($(w-n)$) ($(w-np)$) ($(w-np)!0.5!(A-c)+(90:0.2)$) ($(A-c)+(45:0.4)$) ($(A-c)+(290:0.6)$) ($(w-sp)!0.5!(A-c)+(335:0.4)$) ($(w-sp)+(45:0.4)$) ($(w-sp)$)}] ($(w-s)$);
				\node (mA-label) at ($(w-n)+(45:0.8)$) {\(\mu'(A)\)};
				
				        
				\draw ($(w-n)+(190:0.2)$) edge[edge,dashed,CornflowerBlue,closed,curve through={ ($(w-np)+(135:0.2)$) ($(w-np)!0.5!(A-c)+(90:0.6)$) ($(A-c)+(45:0.6)$) ($(SA-b)+(315:0.6)$) ($(mpAp-y)+(325:0.5)$) (mpAp-y)}] ($(w-s)+(190:0.2)$);
				\node[CornflowerBlue] (mAp-label) at ($(mpAp-y)+(45:1)$) {\(\mu'(A')\)};
				
				    
				\draw ($(A-c)+(110:0.4)$) edge[edge,LavenderMagenta,closed,curve through={($(A-c)+(45:0.3)$) ($(A-c)!0.5!(SA-b)+(45:0.7)$) ($(SA-b)+(45:0.5)$) ($(SA-b)+(315:0.3)$) ($(SA-b)+(225:0.5)$)}] ($(A-c)+(225:0.5)$);
				\node[LavenderMagenta] at ($(A-c)!0.8!(SA-b)+(180:0.1)$) {\(S\cap A'\)};
				
				   
				\draw ($(w)+(45:0.1)$) edge[edge,violet,closed,curve through={ ($(A-x)$) ($(mpAp-y)+(45:0.2)$) ($(mpAp-y)+(315:0.3)$) ($(E-b)!0.5!(Ep-c)$) ($(Ep-c)+(270:0.2)$) ($(Ep-b)+(90:0.1)$) ($(Ep-a)+(270:0.2)$) ($(Ep-a)+(150:0.2)$) ($(A-a)+(135:0.6)$) ($(zA)+(90:1.5)$) ($(w)+(135:0.2)$)}] ($(w)+(90:0.1)$);
				\node[violet] (Ep-label) at ($(Ap-z)+(0:1)$) {\(E'\)};
				
				  
				\draw ($(w)+(45:0.2)$) edge[edge,PastelOrange,closed,curve through={ ($(A-x)+(45:0.1)$) ($(mpAp-y)+(45:0.3)$) ($(mpAp-y)+(315:0.4)$) ($(E-b)$) ($(Ep-c)+(270:0.4)$) ($(E-a)$) ($(A-a)+(135:0.8)$) ($(zA)+(90:1.7)$)}] ($(w)+(90:0.2)$);
				\node[PastelOrange] (E-label) at ($(E-a)+(10:0.4)$) {\(E\)};
				
				      
				\draw[edge,AppleGreen,out=200,in=80] (w) to (zA);
				\draw[directededge,AppleGreen,out=250,in=30] (zA) to (zAp);
				
				\draw[directededge,AppleGreen,bend left] (zAp) to (Ep-c.center);
				
				\draw[directededge,PastelOrange,out=135,in=315] (zAp) to (w);
				
				\node[AppleGreen] (P) at ($(zA)!0.5!(zAp)+(180:0.3)$) {\(P\)};
				\node[PastelOrange] (Q) at ($(mpAp-y)+(180:1.1)$) {\(Q\)};
			\end{tikzpicture}
			\caption{Depiction of objects used in the proof of~\cref{claim:potato_nesting}.}
      \label{fig:potato_nesting}
		\end{figure}
    Refer to \cref{fig:potato_nesting} for an illustration of the objects used in this proof.
    
		It suffices to prove that \(\mu'(A) \subseteq \mu'(A')\).
		Without loss of generality, let us assume that \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}
                     
	\fi} = \Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\).
		By the property achieved in~\cref{potlemma:claim:same_orientation_laminar}, we have \(A \subseteq A'\).
		
		For the sake of contradiction, suppose that there exists a vertex \(w \in \mu'(A) - \mu'(A')\).
		Note that this in particular means that \(w \in A \subseteq A'\).
		Since \(w \not \in \mu'(A')\), there must be (as per definition of \(\mu'(A')\))
		an element \(z' \in Z(A') - (S \cap A')\) and a $k$-key cut \((E,F) \in \mathcal{C}_{A'}(\Set{z'})\),
		where \(S \cap A' \subseteq F\), such that
		\(w \in \ensuremath{\text{closure}_{D[E]}\Paren*{Z(A') \cap E}}\).
		As \(w \in \ensuremath{\text{closure}_{D[E]}\Paren*{Z(A')}}\), there are two paths \(P\) and \(Q\), leading from \(w\) to some \(z_{A'} \in Z(A')\) and back to $w$, respectively, that are entirely contained within \(D[E]\).
		Consider a path $P'$ obtained by extending \(P\) by an edge exiting \(A'\) whose tail is \(z_{A'}\).
		Then $P'$ starts in \(w\) and ends outside of \(A'\) which is a superset of \(A\).
		Hence, $P'$ must pass through a vertex from \(Z(A)\), and this vertex cannot be the
		last vertex of $P'$.
		Call this vertex \(z_A\).
		Since \(z_A \in E\) and \(S \cap A \subseteq S \cap A' \subseteq F\), it follows that \(z_A \not \in S \cap A\).
		Observe then, that \((E,F)\) is an \(\Set{z_A}\)-\((S \cap A)\)-cut of order at most \(k\).
		If \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax E\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}
                     
	\fi} \leqslant k\), then let \(E'\) be the set of all
		vertices reachable from \(z_A\) within \(D[E]\); and if \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax E\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}
                     
	\fi} \leqslant k\),
		then let \(E'\) be the set of all vertices that can reach \(z_A\) within \(D[E]\).
		Note that the order of the cut \((E', V(D) - E')\) is still at most $k$: in the first case we have \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax E'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( E' \right)}{( E' )}{( E' )}{( E' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E' \right)}{( E' )}{( E' )}{( E' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E' \right)}{( E' )}{( E' )}{( E' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E' \right)}{( E' )}{( E' )}{( E' )}}
                     
	\fi}\leqslant \Abs{
	\ifx\relax E\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}
                     
	\fi} \leqslant k\) and in the second case we have \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax E'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( E' \right)}{( E' )}{( E' )}{( E' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E' \right)}{( E' )}{( E' )}{( E' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E' \right)}{( E' )}{( E' )}{( E' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E' \right)}{( E' )}{( E' )}{( E' )}}
                     
	\fi}\leqslant \Abs{
	\ifx\relax E\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}
                     
	\fi} \leqslant k\). Moreover, regardless of the orientation of the cut \((E,F)\),
		the paths \(P\), \(Q\) are contained within \(D[E']\); in particular \(w \in E'\).
		Let \((E'', F'') \in \mathcal{C}_{A}(\Set{z_A})\) be a $k$-key \(\Set{z_A}\)-\((S \cap A)\)-cut
		that extends \((E', V(D) - E')\) (so with $E''\supseteq E'$).
		The paths \(P, Q\) witness that \(w \in \ensuremath{\text{closure}_{D[E]}\Paren*{\Set{z_A}}} \subseteq \ensuremath{\text{closure}_{D[E'']}\Paren*{Z(A) \cap E''}}\).
		But this implies that \(w \not \in \mu'(A)\), which contradicts our assumption.
	\end{claimproof}
	
	Finally, we can define a family of cuts that is globally laminar and of bounded  porosity.
	Consider any \((A,B) \in \mathcal{F}\).
	Let \(\mathcal{A} \subseteq \mathcal{F}\) be the set of all ancestors of \((A,B)\) in the tree \(T\).
	(In particular \((A,B) \in \mathcal{A}\), and $s\notin A'$ for each $(A',B')\in \mathcal{A}$.)
	We define
	\begin{equation*}
		\sigma(A) \coloneqq \bigcap_{(A',B') \in \mathcal{A}} \mu(A')\text{.}
	\end{equation*}
	It might seem at first glance that the set \(\sigma(A)\) is unwieldy to work with,
	as it is defined as an intersection of an unbounded number of sets.
	However, thanks to~\cref{claim:potato_nesting}, each set \(\sigma(A)\) is an intersection of at most two sets of the form \(\mu(A')\).
	We use this observation to establish a bound on the porosity of the cuts imposed by the sets $\sigma(A)$.
	
	\begin{claim}\label{cl:sigma-porosity}
		For every cut $(A,B)\in \mathcal{F}$, the porosity of the cut $(\sigma(A),V(D)- \sigma(A))$ is at most \(k^2 \cdot 4^{k + 1} + 2k\).
	\end{claim}
	\begin{claimproof}
		Let $\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{out}}\coloneqq \{(A',B')\in \mathcal{A} \mid \Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi}=k\}$ and\mbox{ 
			$\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{in}}\coloneqq \{(A',B')\in \mathcal{A} \mid \Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi}=k\}$.}
		Observe that by \cref{claim:potato_nesting}, all the elements of the family $\{\mu(A')\colon (A',B')\in \mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{out}}\}$ are pairwise comparable by inclusion; and the same also holds for the family $\{\mu(A')\colon (A',B')\in \mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{in}}\}$.
		Therefore, $\sigma(A)$ is the intersection of at most two sets of the form $\mu(A')$ for some $(A',B')\in \mathcal{A}$.
		As by~\cref{claim:potato_cycle_order}, each such cut $(\mu(A'),V(D)- \mu(A')))$ has porosity at most \(2k^2 \cdot 4^{k} + k\), it follows from~\cref{lemma:porosity-boolean} that the porosity of $(\sigma(A),V(D)- \sigma(A))$ is at most \(4k^2 \cdot 4^k + 2k=k^2 \cdot 4^{k + 1} + 2k\).
	\end{claimproof}
	
	
	                                                                                     
	                                                                             
	                        
	                                                                                
	                                                                               
	                         
	                                                                  
	                                                                          
	          
	
	For a cut $(A,B)\in \mathcal{F}$, we define $\sigma((A,B))\coloneqq (\sigma(A),V(D)- \sigma(A))$.
	Note that by construction we have $S\cap A=S\cap \sigma(A)$, hence $(A,B)|_S=\sigma((A,B))|_S$.
	Therefore, if we define
	\begin{equation*}
		\sigma(\mathcal{F})\coloneqq \{\sigma((A,B))\colon (A,B)\in \mathcal{F}\},
	\end{equation*}
	then $\sigma(\mathcal{F})|_S=\mathcal{F}|_S$.
	In particular, $\sigma(\mathcal{F})|_S$ is laminar on $S$ and we have that $\mathsf{LaminarTree}(\mathcal{F}|_S,s) = \mathsf{LaminarTree}(\sigma(\mathcal{F})|_S,s)$.
	Additionally, the family of cuts $\sigma(\mathcal{F})$ is globally laminar.
	
	\begin{claim}\label{cl:sigma-laminar}
		$\sigma(\mathcal{F})$ is a laminar family of cuts of $D$.
	\end{claim}
	\begin{claimproof}
		Consider any two cuts $(A,B),(A',B')\in \mathcal{F}$.
		If these cuts are comparable in $T$, say $(A',B')$ is an ancestor of $(A,B)$, then by construction we have $\sigma(A)\subseteq \sigma(A')$.
		On the other hand, if $(A,B)$ and $(A',B')$ are incomparable in $T$, then, by~\cref{cl:incomparable}, it follows that $\sigma(A)$ and $\sigma(A')$ are disjoint.
	\end{claimproof}
	
	Thus we can finally define the mapping \(\eta\) by the following formula.
	                                                              
	                                                        
	               
  For any edge \(e \in E(T)\), let \((X,Y) = \eta'(e)\),
  and let \((A,B)\) be the unique element of \(\mathcal{F}\)
  such that \(A \cap S = X \cap S\). We define
	\begin{equation*}
		\eta(e) \coloneqq \sigma((A,B))= (\sigma(A), V(D) - \sigma(A))
	\end{equation*}
	to obtain the demanded properties.
<!-- end proof-12 -->
<!-- claim-3: body_md -->
\label{potlemma:claim:same_orientation_laminar}
		There exists a family \(\mathcal{F}\) of cuts of $D$ of order \(k\) and a bijection \(\nu: \mathcal{F}' \to \mathcal{F}\), such that
		\begin{itemize}
			                                                                                                     
			                                                                   
			\item for every \((A,B) \in \mathcal{F}'\), the cut \((A_{\mathsf{new}}, B_{\mathsf{new}}) = \nu((A,B))\in \mathcal{F}\)
			fulfils
			\begin{equation*}
				A \cap S = A_{\mathsf{new}} \cap S\qquad\text{and}\qquad B \cap S = B_{\mathsf{new}} \cap S, \text{ and}
			\end{equation*}
			                                                                                                          
			\item for all \((A, B),(A', B') \in \mathcal{F}\),                                    
			where \(\nu^{-1}((A,B))\) is a descendant of \(\nu^{-1}((A',B'))\) in \(T\),
			\begin{itemize}
				\item if \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}
                     
	\fi} = \Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\), then \(A \subseteq A'\); and
				\item if \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}
                     
	\fi} = \Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\), then \(A \subseteq A'\).
			\end{itemize}
		\end{itemize}
<!-- end claim-3 -->
<!-- claimproof-3: body_md -->
Consider any \((A,B) \in \mathcal{F}'\).
		Assume first that \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\).
		Let \(\mathcal{A}\) be the set of all ancestors \((A',B')\) of \((A,B)\) in \(T = T'\) such that
		\(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\) as well. ($\mathcal{A}$ includes $(A,B)$ as well.)
		We set
		\begin{equation*}
			A_{\mathsf{new}} \coloneqq \bigcap_{(A',B') \in \mathcal{A}} A'\qquad\textrm{and}\qquad
			B_{\mathsf{new}} \coloneqq V(D) - A_{\mathsf{new}} \qquad\textrm{and}\qquad \nu((A,B))) \coloneqq (A_{\mathsf{new}},B_{\mathsf{new}}).
		\end{equation*}
		In case that \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\), we use a symmetric definition, but take $\mathcal{A}$ to be the set of all ancestors $(A',B')$ of $(A,B)$ satisfying \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\).
		
		We next show that \((A_{\mathsf{new}}, B_{\mathsf{new}})\) is a cut of order \(k\).
		To that end it suffices to work with a single ancestor \((A',B')\) of \((A,B)\), and to assume by symmetry that \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}
                     
	\fi}=\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\).
		By submodularity,
		\begin{equation*}
			\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A \cup A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \cup A' \right)}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \cup A' \right)}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \cup A' \right)}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \cup A' \right)}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}}
                     
	\fi} + \Abs{
	\ifx\relax A \cap A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \cap A' \right)}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \cap A' \right)}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \cap A' \right)}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \cap A' \right)}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}}
                     
	\fi} \leqslant \Abs{
	\ifx\relax A\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}
                     
	\fi} + \Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi} = 2k.
		\end{equation*}
		Since \(\mathcal{F}'\) is laminar on \(S\), and \((A',B')\) is an ancestor of \((A,B)\),
		it follows that \((A \cup A') \cap S = A' \cap S\) and \(A \cap A' \cap S = A \cap S\).
		As a laminar cut family does not contain trivial cuts, we have
		\(A' \cap S \neq S\), \(A' \cap S \neq \emptyset\) and
		\(A \cap S \neq S\), \(A \cap S \neq \emptyset\).
		Therefore, as \(S\) is \(k\)-flow-connected, neither \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A \cup A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \cup A' \right)}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \cup A' \right)}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \cup A' \right)}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \cup A' \right)}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}}
                     
	\fi}\)
		nor \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A \cap A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \cap A' \right)}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \cap A' \right)}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \cap A' \right)}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \cap A' \right)}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}}
                     
	\fi}\) can be smaller than \(k\).
		Thus, from the inequality above it follows that, actually, \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A \cup A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \cup A' \right)}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \cup A' \right)}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \cup A' \right)}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \cup A' \right)}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}{( A \cup A' )}}
                     
	\fi}=\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A \cap A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \cap A' \right)}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \cap A' \right)}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \cap A' \right)}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \cap A' \right)}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}{( A \cap A' )}}
                     
	\fi}=k\).
		By applying this reasoning to each cut of $\mathcal{A}$ consecutively, we conclude that $\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A_\mathsf{new}\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A_\mathsf{new} \right)}{( A_\mathsf{new} )}{( A_\mathsf{new} )}{( A_\mathsf{new} )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A_\mathsf{new} \right)}{( A_\mathsf{new} )}{( A_\mathsf{new} )}{( A_\mathsf{new} )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A_\mathsf{new} \right)}{( A_\mathsf{new} )}{( A_\mathsf{new} )}{( A_\mathsf{new} )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A_\mathsf{new} \right)}{( A_\mathsf{new} )}{( A_\mathsf{new} )}{( A_\mathsf{new} )}}
                     
	\fi} = k$, as required.
		
		In particular the above argument also shows \(A \cap S = A_{\mathsf{new}} \cap S\),
		and \(B \cap S = B_{\mathsf{new}} \cap S\).
		Now let \(\nu((A, B)) = (A_{\mathsf{new}},B_{\mathsf{new}}),\nu((A', B')) = (A'_{\mathsf{new}},B'_{\mathsf{new}}) \in \mathcal{F}\), where \((A,B)\) is a descendant of \((A',B')\) in \(T\).
		If \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A_{\mathsf{new}}\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A_{\mathsf{new}} \right)}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A_{\mathsf{new}} \right)}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A_{\mathsf{new}} \right)}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A_{\mathsf{new}} \right)}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}}
                     
	\fi} = \Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'_{\mathsf{new}}\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A'_{\mathsf{new}} \right)}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}} )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A'_{\mathsf{new}} \right)}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}} )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A'_{\mathsf{new}} \right)}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}} )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A'_{\mathsf{new}} \right)}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}} )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\) or \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A_{\mathsf{new}}\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A_{\mathsf{new}} \right)}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A_{\mathsf{new}} \right)}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A_{\mathsf{new}} \right)}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A_{\mathsf{new}} \right)}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}{( A_{\mathsf{new}} )}}
                     
	\fi} = \Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'_{\mathsf{new}}\}\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A'_{\mathsf{new}}\} \right)}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}}\} )}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}}\} )}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}}\} )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A'_{\mathsf{new}}\} \right)}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}}\} )}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}}\} )}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}}\} )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A'_{\mathsf{new}}\} \right)}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}}\} )}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}}\} )}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}}\} )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A'_{\mathsf{new}}\} \right)}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}}\} )}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}}\} )}{( A'_{\mathsf{new}}\} )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\),
		then the set \(\mathcal{A}\) of ancestors used for obtaining \(A_{\mathsf{new}}\) is a subset of the set \(\mathcal{A}'\) used for obtaining \(A'_{\mathsf{new}}\).
		Thus, \(A_{\mathsf{new}} \subseteq A'_{\mathsf{new}}\).
<!-- end claimproof-3 -->
<!-- claim-4: body_md -->
\label{claim:potato_cycle_order}
		The porosity of the cut \((\mu(A), V(D) - \mu(A))\) is at most \(2 k^2 \cdot 4^k + k\).
<!-- end claim-4 -->
<!-- claimproof-4: body_md -->
By~\cref{coro:key_cuts}, \((\mu'(A), V(D) - \mu'(A))\)
		is a boolean combination of at most \( 2 k \cdot 4^k + 1\) different cuts,
		each of order at most \(k\), and thus also of porosity at most \(k\).
		So by~\cref{lemma:porosity-boolean}, the porosity of
		\((\mu'(A), V(D)- \mu'(A))\) is at most \(2 k^2 \cdot 4^k + k\).
		Finally, by \cref{lemma:porosity-onecl}, the claim follows.
<!-- end claimproof-4 -->
<!-- claim-5: body_md -->
\label{cl:incomparable}
		If two cuts \((A,B), (A',B') \in \mathcal{F}\)                                             
		are incomparable in~\(T\), then \(\mu(A) \cap \mu(A') = \emptyset\).
<!-- end claim-5 -->
<!-- claimproof-5: body_md -->
\begin{figure}[h!]
			\centering
                                                           
			\begin{tikzpicture}[scale=1.2]
				  
				\node (A) at (-2.5,0) {};
				\draw ($(A)+(0:2.9)$) edge[edge,closed,curve through={($(A)+(30:3)$) ($(A)+(90:2)$) ($(A)+(150:3)$) ($(A)+(180:2.7)$) ($(A)+(210:3)$) ($(A)+(270:2)$)}] ($(A)+(-30:3)$);
				\node (A-label) at ($(A)+(90:2.5)$) {\(A\)};
				
				   
				\node (Ap) at (2.5,0) {};
				\draw ($(Ap)+(0:2.7)$) edge[edge,CornflowerBlue,closed,curve through={($(Ap)+(30:3)$) ($(Ap)+(90:2)$) ($(Ap)+(150:3)$) ($(Ap)+(180:2.9)$) ($(Ap)+(210:3)$) ($(Ap)+(270:2)$)}] ($(Ap)+(330:3)$);
				\node[CornflowerBlue] (Ap-label) at ($(Ap)+(90:2.5)$) {\(A'\)};
				
				\node[vertex,violet] (v) at ($(A)!0.5!(Ap)$) {};
				\node[violet] (v-label) at ($(v)+(240:0.4)$) {\(v\)};
				
				       
				\node (mpA-a) at ($(A)+(90:1.3)$) {};
				\node (mpA-b) at ($(A)+(160:1)$) {};
				\node (mpA-c) at ($(A)+(200:2)$) {};
				\draw ($(A)+(0:2.9)$) edge[edge,closed,curve through={($(A)+(30:3)$) ($(A)+(90:2)$) ($(A)+(150:3)$) ($(A)+(180:2.7)$) ($(A)+(210:3)$) ($(A)+(270:2)$)}] ($(A)+(-30:3)$);
				\node (A-label) at ($(A)+(90:2.5)$) {\(A\)};
				\node[LavenderMagenta] (SA) at ($(mpA-b)!0.5!(mpA-c)$) {\(S\cap A\)};
				\draw ($(SA)+(0:0.7)$) edge[edge,LavenderMagenta,closed,curve through={($(SA)+(45:1)$) ($(SA)+(90:0.7)$) ($(SA)+(135:0.7)$) ($(SA)+(180:0.7)$) ($(SA)+(225:1)$) ($(SA)+(270:0.7)$)}] ($(SA)+(315:0.7)$);
				\draw ($(mpA-a)+(90:0.4)$) edge[edge,dashed,closed,curve through={($(mpA-a)+(0:0.4)$) ($(v)+(90:0.4)$) ($(v)+(0:0.2)$) ($(v)+(270:0.4)$) ($(mpA-b)+(270:1.3)$) ($(mpA-c)+(270:0.7)$) ($(mpA-c)+(180:0.7)$)}] ($(mpA-b)+(90:1)$);
				
				\node (mpA-label) at ($(A)+(260:1.2)$) {\(\mu'(A)\)};
				
				  
				\node[vertex,AppleGreen] (w) at ($(Ap)+(20:2)$) {};
				\node[AppleGreen] (w-label) at ($(w)+(90:0.3)$) {\(w\)};
				
				\node[vertex,AppleGreen] (z) at ($(v)+(70:0.6)$) {};
				\node (zp) at ($(z)+(350:0.6)$) {};
				\node[AppleGreen] (z-label) at ($(z)+(280:0.3)$) {\(z\)};
				
				    
				\node (App-a) at ($(Ap)+(140:1.3)$) {};
				\node (App-b) at ($(Ap)+(240:1.5)$) {};
				\node[violet] (App-label) at ($(App-b)$) {\(A''\)};
				\draw ($(App-a)+(90:0.4)$) edge[edge,violet,closed,curve through={($(App-a)!0.5!(w)$) ($(w)+(90:0.65)$) ($(w)+(0:0.4)$) ($(w)+(270:0.6)$) ($(App-b)+(10:0.3)$) ($(App-b)+(270:0.3)$) ($(App-b)+(190:0.3)$) ($(v)+(270:0.3)$) ($(v)+(180:0.3)$) ($(z)+(130:0.3)$) ($(z)+(90:0.4)$)}] ($(App-a)+(135:0.6)$);
				
				
				        
				\node (mpAp-c) at ($(w)+(260:2)$) {};
				\node (mpAp-b) at ($(mpAp-c)+(200:1)$) {};
				\node (mpAp-a) at ($(mpAp-b)+(220:1.3)$) {};
				\draw ($(w)+(90:0.8)$) edge[edge,CornflowerBlue,dashed,closed,curve through={($(w)+(0:0.7)$) ($(w)!0.5!(mpAp-c)+(315:0.9)$) ($(mpAp-c)+(350:0.7)$) ($(mpAp-c)+(270:0.6)$) ($(mpAp-b)+(270:0.2)$) ($(mpAp-b)+(180:0.2)$) ($(App-b)+(70:0.3)$) ($(v)+(270:0.2)$) ($(v)+(180:0.2)$) ($(z)+(130:0.2)$) ($(z)+(90:0.2)$)}] ($(App-a)$);
				\node[CornflowerBlue] (mpAp-label) at ($(mpAp-b)+(100:0.5)$) {\(\mu'(A')\)};
				
				    
				\draw ($(w)+(90:0.6)$) edge[edge,LavenderMagenta,closed,curve through={($(w)+(0:0.5)$) ($(w)!0.5!(mpAp-c)+(315:0.7)$) ($(mpAp-c)+(350:0.4)$) ($(mpAp-c)+(250:0.3)$) ($(mpAp-c)+(160:0.5)$)}] ($(w)+(180:0.5)$);
				
				
				  
				\node (E-d) at ($(v)+(250:1)$) {};
				\node[PastelOrange] (E-label) at ($(App-b)+(200:1)$) {\(E\)};
				\draw ($(w)+(90:0.8)$) edge[edge,PastelOrange,closed,curve through={($(w)+(0:0.6)$) ($(w)+(270:0.75)$) ($(mpAp-b)+(350:0.2)$) ($(mpAp-a)+(0:0.5)$) ($(mpAp-a)+(270:0.2)$) ($(mpAp-a)+(190:0.3)$) ($(App-b)+(280:0.7)$) ($(App-b)+(225:0.7)$) ($(App-b)+(170:0.7)$) ($(E-d)+(270:0.2)$) ($(E-d)+(180:0.2)$) ($(z)+(130:0.5)$) }] ($(App-a)+(90:0.8)$);
				
				
				      
				\draw[edge,AppleGreen,out=45,in=170] (v) to (z);
				\draw[directededge,AppleGreen,out=350,in=180] (z) to (zp.east);
				\draw (zp) edge[edge,AppleGreen,quick curve through={($(zp)+(0:0.2)$) .. ($(v)!0.5!(Ap)+(90:0.3)$) .. ($(Ap)+(90:0.6)$) .. ($(w)!0.5!(Ap)+(90:0.3)$)}] (w);
				\draw[directededge,AppleGreen] ($(w)+(185:0.3)$) to (w);
				
				\draw (w) edge[edge,PastelOrange,quick curve through={($(w)+(150:0.4)$) .. ($(Ap)$) .. ($(v)+(350:0.7)$)}] (v.east);
				\draw[directededge,PastelOrange] ($(v)+(350:0.3)$) to (v);
				
				\node[LavenderMagenta,fill=white,rounded corners] (SAp) at ($(mpAp-c)+(82:0.6)$) {\(S\cap A'\)};
				
				\node[PastelOrange] (Q) at ($(Ap)+(315:0.3)$) {\(Q\)};
			\end{tikzpicture}
			\caption{Depiction of objects used in the proof of~\cref{cl:incomparable}.}
			\label{fig:incomparable}
		\end{figure}
		Refer to~\cref{fig:incomparable} for an illustration of the objects used in this proof.
		
		Suppose for the sake of contradiction that \(v\) is an element of \(\mu(A) \cap \mu(A')\).
		Without loss of generality, assume that \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\).
		From the definition of closure,
		it follows that there is a path \(P\) from \(v\) to an element \(w \in S \cap A'\) contained
		entirely within \(D[\mu'(A')]\) and a path \(Q\) from \(w\) to \(v\) contained
		entirely within \(D[\mu'(A')]\).
		Since \(S \cap A \cap A' = \emptyset\) (as $(A,B),(A',B')$ are incomparable in the tree \(T\)),
		and \(v \in \mu(A) \subseteq A\), the path \(P\) starts in a vertex
		of \(A\), and ends in a vertex not in \(A\). So it must contain a vertex
		\(z \in Z(A)\). Note that we have \(z \not \in S \cap A\), as \(P\)
		is contained within \(\mu'(A') \subseteq A'\) and \(A' \cap S \cap A = \emptyset\).
		
		
		Observe that \((A',B')\) is a \(\Set{z}\)-\((S \cap A)\)-cut, as \(z \in A'\) and \(S \cap A \cap A' = \emptyset\). 
		Define $A''$ to be:
		\begin{itemize}
			\item  the set of all vertices reachable from \(\Set{z}\) within \(D[A']\), if \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\);
			\item the set of all vertices that can reach \(\Set{z}\) within \(D[A']\), if \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\).
		\end{itemize}
		In either case we have $\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A''\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A'' \right)}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A'' \right)}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A'' \right)}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A'' \right)}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}}
                     
	\fi} \leqslant k$ or $\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A''\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A'' \right)}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A'' \right)}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A'' \right)}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A'' \right)}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}{( A'' )}}
                     
	\fi} \leqslant k$, respectively, and
		$(A'',V(D)- A'')$ is still a \(\Set{z}\)-\((S \cap A)\)-cut. Moreover, the paths
		\(P\) and \(Q\) are contained within \(D[A'']\) regardless of the orientation
		of the cut \((A', B')\); in particular \(v \in A''\).
		
		Let \((E,F)\) be a $k$-key \(\Set{z}\)-\((S \cap A)\)-cut that
		extends the cut \((A'', V(D) - A'')\) (so with \(A'' \subseteq E\)).
		The paths \(P\) and \(Q\) show that \(v\) belongs to
		\(\ensuremath{\text{closure}_{D[E]}\Paren*{\set{z}}}\), and so \(v\) does
		not belong to \(\mu'(A)\) by construction of $\mu'(A)$. This is a contradiction.
<!-- end claimproof-5 -->
<!-- claim-6: body_md -->
\label{claim:potato_nesting}
		Let \((A,B), (A',B') \in \mathcal{F}\).                                             
		If \((A,B)\) is a descendant of \((A', B')\) in \(T\), and
		\(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}
                     
	\fi} = \Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\) or \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}
                     
	\fi} = \Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\),
		then \(\mu(A) \subseteq \mu(A')\).
<!-- end claim-6 -->
<!-- claimproof-6: body_md -->
\begin{figure}[!ht]
			\centering
                                                             
			\begin{tikzpicture}
				\node (Ap) at (0,0) {};
				
				\node[vertex,AppleGreen] (zA) at ($(Ap)+(200:1)$) {};
				\node[AppleGreen] (zA-label) at ($(zA)+(180:0.4)$) {\(z_A\)};
				
				\node[vertex,AppleGreen] (zAp) at ($(Ap)+(270:3)$) {};
				\node[AppleGreen] (zAp-label) at ($(zAp)+(190:0.4)$) {\(z_{A'}\)};
				
				\node (Ap-z) at ($(Ap)+(190:4)$) {};
				
				\node[vertex,violet] (w) at ($(Ap)+(80:1)$) {};
				\node[violet] (w-label) at ($(w)+(30:0.5)$) {\(w\)};
				\node (w-n) at ($(w)+(70:0.6)$) {};
				\node (w-s) at ($(w)+(0:0.6)$) {};
				\node (w-np) at ($(w-n)+(100:1.4)$) {};
				\node (w-sp) at ($(w-s)+(335:0.6)$) {};
				
				  
				\node (A-a) at ($(zA)+(170:0.7)$) {};
				\node (A-x) at ($(Ap)+(355:1)$) {};
				\node (A-b) at ($(w)+(135:2)$) {};
				\node (A-c) at ($(w)+(40:3)$) {};
				
				   
				\node (Ep-c) at ($(zAp)+(270:0.7)$) {};
				\node (Ep-b) at ($(Ep-c)+(180:1)$) {};
				\node (Ep-a) at ($(Ep-b)+(170:1)$) {};
				
				  
				\node (E-a) at ($(Ap-z)+(225:2)$) {};
				\node (E-b) at ($(Ep-c)+(355:2)$) {};
				
				    
				\node (SA-b) at ($(A-c)+(290:2)$) {};
				
				     
				\node (mpAp-y) at ($(A-x)+(315:1)$) {};
				
				         
				\draw ($(zAp)+(270:0.4)$) edge[edge,CornflowerBlue,closed,curve through={ ($(Ep-b)+(45:0.4)$) ($(Ep-a)+(45:0.4)$) ($(Ap-z)+(200:0.4)$) ($(A-b)+(135:0.6)$) ($(A-c)+(30:1)$)}] ($(SA-b)+(0:1)$);
				\node[CornflowerBlue] (Ap-label) at ($(A-c)+(45:1.3)$) {\(A'\)};
				
				        
				\draw ($(Ap)+(270:0.4)$) edge[edge,closed,curve through={ ($(zA)+(270:0.4)$) ($(A-a)+(225:0.4)$) ($(A-b)+(135:0.4)$) ($(A-b)!0.5!(A-c)+(90:0.8)$) ($(A-c)+(30:0.6)$) ($(w-sp)+(335:0.2)$)}] (A-x);
				\node (A-label) at ($(A-a)+(90:2)$) {\(A\)};
				
				       
				\draw ($(w)+(225:0.3)$) edge[edge,dashed,closed,curve through={ ($(w-n)$) ($(w-np)$) ($(w-np)!0.5!(A-c)+(90:0.2)$) ($(A-c)+(45:0.4)$) ($(A-c)+(290:0.6)$) ($(w-sp)!0.5!(A-c)+(335:0.4)$) ($(w-sp)+(45:0.4)$) ($(w-sp)$)}] ($(w-s)$);
				\node (mA-label) at ($(w-n)+(45:0.8)$) {\(\mu'(A)\)};
				
				        
				\draw ($(w-n)+(190:0.2)$) edge[edge,dashed,CornflowerBlue,closed,curve through={ ($(w-np)+(135:0.2)$) ($(w-np)!0.5!(A-c)+(90:0.6)$) ($(A-c)+(45:0.6)$) ($(SA-b)+(315:0.6)$) ($(mpAp-y)+(325:0.5)$) (mpAp-y)}] ($(w-s)+(190:0.2)$);
				\node[CornflowerBlue] (mAp-label) at ($(mpAp-y)+(45:1)$) {\(\mu'(A')\)};
				
				    
				\draw ($(A-c)+(110:0.4)$) edge[edge,LavenderMagenta,closed,curve through={($(A-c)+(45:0.3)$) ($(A-c)!0.5!(SA-b)+(45:0.7)$) ($(SA-b)+(45:0.5)$) ($(SA-b)+(315:0.3)$) ($(SA-b)+(225:0.5)$)}] ($(A-c)+(225:0.5)$);
				\node[LavenderMagenta] at ($(A-c)!0.8!(SA-b)+(180:0.1)$) {\(S\cap A'\)};
				
				   
				\draw ($(w)+(45:0.1)$) edge[edge,violet,closed,curve through={ ($(A-x)$) ($(mpAp-y)+(45:0.2)$) ($(mpAp-y)+(315:0.3)$) ($(E-b)!0.5!(Ep-c)$) ($(Ep-c)+(270:0.2)$) ($(Ep-b)+(90:0.1)$) ($(Ep-a)+(270:0.2)$) ($(Ep-a)+(150:0.2)$) ($(A-a)+(135:0.6)$) ($(zA)+(90:1.5)$) ($(w)+(135:0.2)$)}] ($(w)+(90:0.1)$);
				\node[violet] (Ep-label) at ($(Ap-z)+(0:1)$) {\(E'\)};
				
				  
				\draw ($(w)+(45:0.2)$) edge[edge,PastelOrange,closed,curve through={ ($(A-x)+(45:0.1)$) ($(mpAp-y)+(45:0.3)$) ($(mpAp-y)+(315:0.4)$) ($(E-b)$) ($(Ep-c)+(270:0.4)$) ($(E-a)$) ($(A-a)+(135:0.8)$) ($(zA)+(90:1.7)$)}] ($(w)+(90:0.2)$);
				\node[PastelOrange] (E-label) at ($(E-a)+(10:0.4)$) {\(E\)};

				\draw[directededge,AppleGreen,out=250,in=30] (zA) to (zAp);
				
				\draw[directededge,AppleGreen,bend left] (zAp) to (Ep-c.center);
				
				\draw[directededge,PastelOrange,out=135,in=315] (zAp) to (w);
				
				\node[AppleGreen] (P) at ($(zA)!0.5!(zAp)+(180:0.3)$) {\(P\)};
				\node[PastelOrange] (Q) at ($(mpAp-y)+(180:1.1)$) {\(Q\)};

			\caption{Depiction of objects used in the proof of~\cref{claim:potato_nesting}.}
      \label{fig:potato_nesting}
		\end{figure}
    Refer to \cref{fig:potato_nesting} for an illustration of the objects used in this proof.
    
		It suffices to prove that \(\mu'(A) \subseteq \mu'(A')\).
		Without loss of generality, let us assume that \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax A\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A \right)}{( A )}{( A )}{( A )}}
                     
	\fi} = \Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi} = k\).
		By the property achieved in~\cref{potlemma:claim:same_orientation_laminar}, we have \(A \subseteq A'\).
		
		For the sake of contradiction, suppose that there exists a vertex \(w \in \mu'(A) - \mu'(A')\).
		Note that this in particular means that \(w \in A \subseteq A'\).
		Since \(w \not \in \mu'(A')\), there must be (as per definition of \(\mu'(A')\))
		an element \(z' \in Z(A') - (S \cap A')\) and a $k$-key cut \((E,F) \in \mathcal{C}_{A'}(\Set{z'})\),
		where \(S \cap A' \subseteq F\), such that
		\(w \in \ensuremath{\text{closure}_{D[E]}\Paren*{Z(A') \cap E}}\).
		As \(w \in \ensuremath{\text{closure}_{D[E]}\Paren*{Z(A')}}\), there are two paths \(P\) and \(Q\), leading from \(w\) to some \(z_{A'} \in Z(A')\) and back to $w$, respectively, that are entirely contained within \(D[E]\).
		Consider a path $P'$ obtained by extending \(P\) by an edge exiting \(A'\) whose tail is \(z_{A'}\).
		Then $P'$ starts in \(w\) and ends outside of \(A'\) which is a superset of \(A\).
		Hence, $P'$ must pass through a vertex from \(Z(A)\), and this vertex cannot be the
		last vertex of $P'$.
		Call this vertex \(z_A\).
		Since \(z_A \in E\) and \(S \cap A \subseteq S \cap A' \subseteq F\), it follows that \(z_A \not \in S \cap A\).
		Observe then, that \((E,F)\) is an \(\Set{z_A}\)-\((S \cap A)\)-cut of order at most \(k\).
		If \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax E\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}
                     
	\fi} \leqslant k\), then let \(E'\) be the set of all
		vertices reachable from \(z_A\) within \(D[E]\); and if \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax E\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}
                     
	\fi} \leqslant k\),
		then let \(E'\) be the set of all vertices that can reach \(z_A\) within \(D[E]\).
		Note that the order of the cut \((E', V(D) - E')\) is still at most $k$: in the first case we have \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax E'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( E' \right)}{( E' )}{( E' )}{( E' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E' \right)}{( E' )}{( E' )}{( E' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E' \right)}{( E' )}{( E' )}{( E' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E' \right)}{( E' )}{( E' )}{( E' )}}
                     
	\fi}\leqslant \Abs{
	\ifx\relax E\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}
                     
	\fi} \leqslant k\) and in the second case we have \(\Abs{
	\ifx\relax E'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( E' \right)}{( E' )}{( E' )}{( E' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E' \right)}{( E' )}{( E' )}{( E' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E' \right)}{( E' )}{( E' )}{( E' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E' \right)}{( E' )}{( E' )}{( E' )}}
                     
	\fi}\leqslant \Abs{
	\ifx\relax E\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( E \right)}{( E )}{( E )}{( E )}}
                     
	\fi} \leqslant k\). Moreover, regardless of the orientation of the cut \((E,F)\),
		the paths \(P\), \(Q\) are contained within \(D[E']\); in particular \(w \in E'\).
		Let \((E'', F'') \in \mathcal{C}_{A}(\Set{z_A})\) be a $k$-key \(\Set{z_A}\)-\((S \cap A)\)-cut
		that extends \((E', V(D) - E')\) (so with $E''\supseteq E'$).
		The paths \(P, Q\) witness that \(w \in \ensuremath{\text{closure}_{D[E]}\Paren*{\Set{z_A}}} \subseteq \ensuremath{\text{closure}_{D[E'']}\Paren*{Z(A) \cap E''}}\).
		But this implies that \(w \not \in \mu'(A)\), which contradicts our assumption.
<!-- end claimproof-6 -->
<!-- claim-7: body_md -->
\label{cl:sigma-porosity}
		For every cut $(A,B)\in \mathcal{F}$, the porosity of the cut $(\sigma(A),V(D)- \sigma(A))$ is at most \(k^2 \cdot 4^{k + 1} + 2k\).
<!-- end claim-7 -->
<!-- claimproof-7: body_md -->
Let $\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{out}}\coloneqq \{(A',B')\in \mathcal{A} \mid \Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{out}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi}=k\}$ and\mbox{ 
			$\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{in}}\coloneqq \{(A',B')\in \mathcal{A} \mid \Abs{
	\ifx\relax A'\relax
		\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\!\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}{\partial^\mathsf{in}_{}\mathchoice{\left( A' \right)}{( A' )}{( A' )}{( A' )}}
                     
	\fi}=k\}$.}
		Observe that by \cref{claim:potato_nesting}, all the elements of the family $\{\mu(A')\colon (A',B')\in \mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{out}}\}$ are pairwise comparable by inclusion; and the same also holds for the family $\{\mu(A')\colon (A',B')\in \mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{in}}\}$.
		Therefore, $\sigma(A)$ is the intersection of at most two sets of the form $\mu(A')$ for some $(A',B')\in \mathcal{A}$.
		As by~\cref{claim:potato_cycle_order}, each such cut $(\mu(A'),V(D)- \mu(A')))$ has porosity at most \(2k^2 \cdot 4^{k} + k\), it follows from~\cref{lemma:porosity-boolean} that the porosity of $(\sigma(A),V(D)- \sigma(A))$ is at most \(4k^2 \cdot 4^k + 2k=k^2 \cdot 4^{k + 1} + 2k\).
<!-- end claimproof-7 -->
<!-- claim-8: body_md -->
\label{cl:sigma-laminar}
		$\sigma(\mathcal{F})$ is a laminar family of cuts of $D$.
<!-- end claim-8 -->
<!-- claimproof-8: body_md -->
Consider any two cuts $(A,B),(A',B')\in \mathcal{F}$.
		If these cuts are comparable in $T$, say $(A',B')$ is an ancestor of $(A,B)$, then by construction we have $\sigma(A)\subseteq \sigma(A')$.
		On the other hand, if $(A,B)$ and $(A',B')$ are incomparable in $T$, then, by~\cref{cl:incomparable}, it follows that $\sigma(A)$ and $\sigma(A')$ are disjoint.
<!-- end claimproof-8 -->
<!-- proof-13: body_md -->
We prove the assertion by induction on the desired flow-connectivity~\(k\).
  The base case is \(k = 1\).
  Let \(D\) be an arbitrary digraph, and let \(\mathcal{C} = \Set{C_1, C_2, \dots, C_\ell}\)
  be the strongly connected components of \(D\).
  Let \(T\) be any rooted tree with \(\ell\) nodes.
  Fix any bijection \(\beta \colon V(T) \to \mathcal{C}\) and let \(\gamma \colon E(T) \ni v \mapsto \emptyset \subseteq E(D)\).
  Clearly, any cycle in \(D\) must be fully contained within only one element of \(\mathcal{C}\).
  Further, any two vertices of a single strongly connected component lie on a common cycle, hence
  every \(C_i \in \mathcal{C}\) is \(1\)-flow-connected.
  Thus, \((T, \beta, \gamma)\) is a \(1\)-flow-connected\xspace tree-partition of porosity\xspace~\(0\).
  Hence we may set \(
	\ifx\relax 1\relax
		\textcolor{green!50!black}{\mathsf{f}_{\ref{lemma:potato}}}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\textcolor{green!50!black}{\mathsf{f}_{\ref{lemma:potato}}}\!\mathchoice{\left( 1 \right)}{( 1 )}{( 1 )}{( 1 )}}{\textcolor{green!50!black}{\mathsf{f}_{\ref{lemma:potato}}}\mathchoice{\left( 1 \right)}{( 1 )}{( 1 )}{( 1 )}}{\textcolor{green!50!black}{\mathsf{f}_{\ref{lemma:potato}}}\mathchoice{\left( 1 \right)}{( 1 )}{( 1 )}{( 1 )}}{\textcolor{green!50!black}{\mathsf{f}_{\ref{lemma:potato}}}\mathchoice{\left( 1 \right)}{( 1 )}{( 1 )}{( 1 )}}
                     
	\fi \coloneqq 0\).

  Now, let us assume that the statement holds for \(k\) and our goal is to prove it for \(k + 1\).
  Let \(D\) be an arbitrary digraph.
  Our goal is to obtain a tree-partition \((T_{\mathsf{fin}}, \beta_{\mathsf{fin}}, \gamma_{\mathsf{fin}})\) of \(D\) where each bag \(\beta_{\mathsf{fin}}(x)\) for $x\in V(T_\mathsf{fin})$ is \((k + 1)\)-flow-connected.
  By induction hypothesis for \(D\) and \(k\) we obtain a $k$-flow-connected\xspace tree-partition~\(\mathcal{T}^{\mathsf{init}}=(T, \beta, \gamma)\) of porosity\xspace at most
  \(
	\ifx\relax k\relax
		\textcolor{green!50!black}{\mathsf{f}_{\ref{lemma:potato}}}
	\else
		\mathchoice{\textcolor{green!50!black}{\mathsf{f}_{\ref{lemma:potato}}}\!\mathchoice{\left( k \right)}{( k )}{( k )}{( k )}}{\textcolor{green!50!black}{\mathsf{f}_{\ref{lemma:potato}}}\mathchoice{\left( k \right)}{( k )}{( k )}{( k )}}{\textcolor{green!50!black}{\mathsf{f}_{\ref{lemma:potato}}}\mathchoice{\left( k \right)}{( k )}{( k )}{( k )}}{\textcolor{green!50!black}{\mathsf{f}_{\ref{lemma:potato}}}\mathchoice{\left( k \right)}{( k )}{( k )}{( k )}}
                     
	\fi\). By rooting $\mathcal{T}^\mathsf{init}$ at any node~$r$ and applying~\cref{lem:rooting_potdecomps}, we may assume that $\mathcal{T}^\mathsf{init}$ is coherent\xspace and rooted at $r$.
  Note that this operation does not break the $k$-flow-connectedness of the bags, for a subset of a $k$-flow-connected set is also $k$-flow-connected, and also does not increase the porosity of the tree-partition.
                                                                                  
                                                                         
  
	
                                                                                                                                             
                
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   
              
  
  For every node \(x \in V(T)\) the set of vertices \(\beta(x)\) is \(k\)-flow-connected by the induction assumption.
  Let \((T_x, \beta_x, \eta'_x)\) be the outcome of \cref{lem:partial_potatoes}.
  Now, we can apply~\cref{lem:partial_potatoes_uncrossed} to obtain a partial tree-partition \((T_x,\beta_x,\eta_x)\) of \(\beta(x)\) in \(D\).
  Thus we obtain \(\set{(T_x, \beta_x, \eta_x) \colon x \in V(T)}\),
  where each \(\beta_x\) has in its image only \((k + 1)\)-flow-connected sets by~\cref{lem:partial_potatoes},
  and each \(\eta_x(E(T_x))\) is a laminar family of cuts consistent with the near-partition \(\beta_x\) by~\cref{lem:partial_potatoes_uncrossed}.

				\node[vertex,CornflowerBlue] (A-6) at ($(A-3)+(275:0.7)$) {};
				\node[vertex,CornflowerBlue] (A-7) at ($(A-4)+(260:0.7)$) {};
				\node[vertex,CornflowerBlue] (A-8) at ($(A-4)+(330:0.7)$) {};

				\node[vertex,CornflowerBlue] (A-10) at ($(A-8)+(270:0.7)$) {};

				\draw[edge,CornflowerBlue] (A-1) to (A-2);

				\draw[edge,CornflowerBlue] (B-1) to (B-2);
				\draw[edge,CornflowerBlue] (B-1) to (B-3);

				 (C-centre) at ($(A-centre)+(270:1)+(330:3)$) {};

				\node[child,CornflowerBlue] (T-B-1) at ($(B-centre)+(210:3)$) {};

				\draw[edge,in=190,out=90,CornflowerBlue] (T-B-2.east) to (B-1);
